# The Idea Book — extract: hunches and docket (family HD)
Written 2026-09-13 from the 15 files below, read in full. No warehouse queries.
Every hunch and every docket line is one IDEAS row. Same question in two files = one row, both cited.
Numbers are as written in the sources. Nothing rounded, nothing invented.

(one smokestack to the global parent and back to the workers) | chain | FRS_FACILITIES, FRS_PROGRAM_LINKS, ECHO, TRI_BASIC_2023, GHGRP_EMISSION, EPA_CORPORATE_CROSSWALK (pivot), GLEIF_RELATIONSHIPS (pivot), GLEIF, XWALK_ZCTA_COUNTY (pivot), QCEW, CDC_INJURY_VIOLENCE_COUNTY | FRS_ID → LEI → parent LEI → country; ZIP → FIPS | tree / chain | not started; 11 layers, 6 turns (the ceiling), ~85% survival | join_depth_ceiling § Chain B |

## METHODS

| id | name | what it is, plain | when to use it | source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| HD-M001 | Three tiers | 1 seeded/live: chain holds, first number, dated legs; 2 one vintage: a leg is a snapshot; 3 dead as written: a leg missing | grade any hunch before spending warehouse money | hunch_matrix_refined § The three tiers; hunch_expansion_32_75 § tiers |
| HD-M002 | Nearest live version | a dead hunch gets the closest chain that does exist, never dropped | every tier-3 hunch | hunch_matrix_refined § Tier 3 |
| HD-M003 | Per-hunch card | refined line, chain that holds, what ran, number, trap | writing up any probe | hunch_matrix_refined § Per-hunch cards |
| HD-M004 | Hub probe + fresh skeptic | one probe agent per hub (county, clinician, facility, cross-hub); each hunch rebuilt by a fresh-context skeptic from the chain text | any batch of hunches; 50 agents, 1,184 tool calls, 82 minutes for 44 | hunch_expansion_32_75 § How it ran |
| HD-M005 | Skeptic rerun discipline | rewrite the SQL independently, check both join keys, entity vs row counts, sentinels, caps, vintages; verdict CONFIRMED/CORRECTED/REFUTED | before any done claim | hunch_expansion_32_75 § Skeptic scorecard; per-hunch cards |
| HD-M006 | Blind spot paragraph | skeptic names what its rerun cannot see because it shares the source tables | every skeptic verdict | hunch_expansion_32_75 § per-hunch cards |
| HD-M007 | Hit means / miss means | state before running what a hit and a miss would each mean | every chain | hunch_expansion_32_75 § per-hunch cards; docket_crossjoin_25 |
| HD-M008 | Carbon-dating a per-NPI file | newest NPPES PROVIDER_ENUMERATION_DATE among NPIs present bounds the data year | any CMS per-NPI load with no year column | hunch_expansion_32_75 § Vintage corrections; #39, #40 |
| HD-M009 | Wish-list scoring | score = 2 × tier-3 rescues + 1 × tier-2 promotions; LIKE-search landing, marts, registry at column level before calling missing | ranking datasets to load | hunch_expansion_32_75 § The wish list; § Method, from the wish agent |
| HD-M010 | Placebo before/after | run the same windows on never-treated units; difference-in-differences is the finding | any before/after on a file with a growth trend | hunch_expansion_32_75 #45, #51 |
| HD-M011 | Clip event dates to the data range | keep only events with both windows inside [file_min+window, file_max-window] | any before/after count | hunch_expansion_32_75 #45 traps, #51 |
| HD-M012 | Balanced panel | compare the same entities in both years, never avg(year A) vs avg(year B) | suppressed or churning cohorts (CDC 1-9) | hunch_expansion_32_75 #52 |
| HD-M013 | Per-pair difference-in-differences | expected_after = sum(before × nat_after/nat_before) per pair, not a pooled ratio-of-ratios | any DiD across pairs with different calendar windows | hunch_expansion_32_75 #50 |
| HD-M014 | Mix standardisation | re-weight year 2 to year 1's complaint/standard survey mix before reading a severity trend | deficiency severity trends | hunch_expansion_32_75 #44 |
| HD-M015 | Within-class comparison | split by ownership type or population band before calling a gap real | any cohort gap (triple owners, jail counties, HUD density) | hunch_expansion_32_75 #46, #55, #67 |
| HD-M016 | Condition on exposure | restrict both arms to entities that were actually inspected before comparing violators to clean | any enforcement-table comparison | hunch_expansion_32_75 #61 |
| HD-M017 | Tie-free percentile | percentile_cont(0.90) within group or rank only non-zero rows instead of ntile | money columns with mass zeros or integer ties | hunch_expansion_32_75 #56, #57, #54 |
| HD-M018 | Denominator by eligible roster | divide dated counts by the roster eligible at each date before reading direction | as-of-today registries (opt-out, exclusions) | hunch_expansion_32_75 #38 |
| HD-M019 | Modification-number truncation check | compare count(*) to max(modification_number) per award | any USASpending per-award count | hunch_expansion_32_75 #75 |
| HD-M020 | Normaliser sanity pair | test the name normaliser on a known pair before trusting a match count | any regexp name join in Snowflake | hunch_expansion_32_75 #68, #70 |
| HD-M021 | Crawl / walk / run | crawl = describe one table; walk = two tables on a key; run = many tables, the card or chain | labelling docket ideas | docket_v2_notes; docket_v2_vs_v1 |
| HD-M022 | Docket seven states | open, partial, confirmed, modest, dead, merged (dead covers no pattern or years do not line up) | status column of any idea list | docket/README.md § The seven ways |
| HD-M023 | Docket data check | per entry: tables exist, row counts, year spans, staleness; only gate one of four | before writing a join | docket/DATA.md; README § Checking |
| HD-M024 | Probe folder self-link | a folder named <id>_<slug> under reports/ links to its entry on rebuild | filing any probe write-up | docket/README.md § Two columns |
| HD-M025 | Turns, not table count | count the times a chain changes what kind of thing it is about; strangers feel a turn, not a seventh join | judging a chain's impressiveness | join_depth_ceiling § What actually makes a chain impressive |
| HD-M026 | Named-example panel | after a cross-key hop, show the nine you could resolve, never a percentage | any chain with a 2-16% hop | join_depth_ceiling § Chain C |
| HD-M027 | Move 1 Absence | the row that should exist and does not: zero inspections, no payment row, no provider | who was never looked at | idea_moves_20 § 1 |
| HD-M028 | Move 2 Precedence | does A reliably precede B; event-study chart with day zero | reactive vs proactive enforcement | idea_moves_20 § 2 |
| HD-M029 | Move 3 Digit tests | Benford on leading digits; haggled numbers bend away | penalty and fine dollars | idea_moves_20 § 3 |
| HD-M030 | Move 4 Impossible zero | round-number clustering at the bottom; zero injuries at 900,000 hours is a claim | self-reported counts | idea_moves_20 § 4 |
| HD-M031 | Move 5 Same entity, two agencies | one EIN telling different stories on different forms | headcounts across 300A, Form 5500, BMF; the warehouse's best structural edge | idea_moves_20 § 5 |
| HD-M032 | Move 6 Disappearance | last year each entity stops filing, then the three years before | HCRIS, revocations, OLMS, water systems | idea_moves_20 § 6 |
| HD-M033 | Move 7 Rebirth | same building, new name; ownership churn against penalty dates | penalised facilities | idea_moves_20 § 7 |
| HD-M034 | Move 8 Address collision | single addresses hosting absurd counts | UK Companies House, ICIJ addresses, NPPES, ATF FFL | idea_moves_20 § 8 |
| HD-M035 | Move 9 Narratives | free text nobody has read; most common verbs by industry | six text tables: OSHA case detail, MSHA accidents, redlining descriptions, CFPB complaints, parentheticals, WPA narratives | idea_moves_20 § 9 |
| HD-M036 | Move 10 Weird for your own kind | outlier inside a matched peer group, not national | Part B billing by specialty/state/patient mix; COHORT_QUEUE shape | idea_moves_20 § 10 |
| HD-M037 | Move 11 Counterfactual twin | match on everything but one variable; paired dumbbells | hospital ownership type | idea_moves_20 § 11 |
| HD-M038 | Move 12 Network position | rank by betweenness not degree; the node whose removal splits the graph | ICIJ relationships 3.3M links, cosponsorship, 13F co-holding | idea_moves_20 § 12 |
| HD-M039 | Move 13 Cadence | when the filing arrives; Friday-night and year-end dumps; clock face or day-of-week heatmap | Federal Register, NHTSA recalls, SEC insider filings | idea_moves_20 § 13 |
| HD-M040 | Move 14 Reciprocity | money that goes both ways in the same cycle | FEC committee-to-candidate | idea_moves_20 § 14 |
| HD-M041 | Move 15 Staleness | acting on old information; map effective date vs storm history; last inspection date | NFIP status book, NID dams | idea_moves_20 § 15 |
| HD-M042 | Move 16 Where the government looks | monitor placement vs population and emissions; questions the denominator of every other chart | AQS sites | idea_moves_20 § 16 |
| HD-M043 | Move 17 Warehouse contradicts itself | duplicate sources disagree; the gap is the chart | FRS pairs, Senate trade sources, NH vs NH411, Retraction Watch copies | idea_moves_20 § 17 |
| HD-M044 | Move 18 Every line in the sand | bunching just below every reporting threshold; one histogram per threshold | PPP $150,000, single-audit trigger, $125 meal line | idea_moves_20 § 18 |
| HD-M045 | Move 19 The week it always happens | seasonal signatures; same calendar week every year | NICS by week, overdose by month, storm damage by season | idea_moves_20 § 19 |
| HD-M046 | Move 20 Rank churn | movement as the subject; bump chart | top pharma payers, counties by overdose, employers by injury | idea_moves_20 § 20 |
| HD-M047 | Visual vocabulary | map, timeline, ranking, flow, other; scrollytelling for chains; sankey for money; bump for rank churn; dumbbell for twins | picking the picture per idea | docket_v2 visual column; idea_moves_20 |

## RULES

| id | rule, plain | why | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| HD-R001 | Steel keys chain as deep as you like: NPI, CCN, FRS_ID, PWSID, NPDES_ID, LEI, CL_PERSON_ID 98–100%; EIN, CIK, FEC ids 86–100% | ten hops still return four rows in five (81.7%) | join_depth_ceiling § The mechanic; The rules |
| HD-R002 | FIPS is a destination, never a doorway; aggregate there and stop | 73–100% per hop, 19.7% after ten hops | join_depth_ceiling § The rules |
| HD-R003 | One cross-key hop (CIK~EIN, EIN~UEI, DUNS~UEI 2–16%) caps the whole chain at a sample; spend it last or never | 8.8% at hop two, 7.5% at hop ten | join_depth_ceiling § The mechanic |
| HD-R004 | UEI, DUNS 10–80%: one hop then stop | sparse keys | join_depth_ceiling § Key class table |
| HD-R005 | NAME@ZIP is 8% real; sample, multi-word names only | single-word name matches collide | join_depth_ceiling § Key class table |
| HD-R006 | Only hard-key crosswalks are materialised (NPI, EIN, CIK, DUNS, CCN, IMO, MMSI, UEI, LEI); code keys never bridge (NAICS, SIC, NCES, DOCKET, PATENT, FIPS, ZIP) | catalog bridge rule | join_depth_ceiling § The mechanic |
| HD-R007 | Every turn must land on one of the twelve bridge tables; six turns is today's ceiling | no seventh bridge exists | join_depth_ceiling § The rules |
| HD-R008 | EIA_PLANT_ID, UTILITY_ID, MSHA ids, FDIC_CERT, IMO/MMSI, CUSIP bridge are in the keyset with zero edges; DOCKET 17 tables, 2 edges | flag as unproven in notes | docket_v2_notes § How the join rule was applied |
| HD-R009 | No org-to-person enrollment bridge is landed: shared ASSOCIATE_ID is org↔org only; FACILITY_AFFILIATION covers hospitals, HHAs, hospices, nursing homes, dialysis, IRF, LTCH and nothing else | kills FQHC, RHC, OTP, MDPP doctor legs; 0 of 11,565 NPIs under FQHC IDs are type 14-xx | hunch_matrix_refined § The bridge that decides seven |
| HD-R010 | LEIE carries a real NPI on 8,841 rows / 8,660 distinct of 83,842; 75,001 hold '0000000000'; every LEIE-join count is a floor over a tenth | a LEIE zero is not evidence of absence | hunch_matrix_refined § Two caveats; hunch_expansion_32_75 #41 traps |
| HD-R011 | Bare try_to_number(x) is NUMBER(38,0) and rounds; use try_to_number(x,18,6) on every average and ratio | 3.5 became 4, spend ratio pointed wrong way, DME dollars read 2.7% low | hunch_matrix_refined § Two caveats; #23, #31 |
| HD-R012 | Nursing-home ownership-change flag PROVIDER_CHANGED_OWNERSHIP_IN_LAST_12_MONTHS is 'N' on all 14,700 rows | constant, not a signal | hunch_matrix_refined #30 |
| HD-R013 | FED_CMS_PECOS_PROVIDER_ENROLLMENT and FED_CMS_MEDICARE_FEE_FOR_SERVICE_PUBLIC_PROVIDER_ENROLLMENT are the same file, equal hash | joining them is a self-join | hunch_matrix_refined #30 |
| HD-R014 | FED_CMS_MEDICARE_PROVIDER is the same file as ..._PHYSICIAN_OTHER_PRACTITIONERS_BY_PROVIDER (hash_agg equal, $120,057,897,699.85 both) | self-join, same shape as PECOS twin | hunch_expansion_32_75 § Found landed; #57 |
| HD-R015 | HCRIS is hospitals only (STH 3,236, CAH 1,382, PH 646, RH 389, LTCH 340, CH 91); no SNF, no HHA | swap SNF money legs to NH411 | hunch_matrix_refined #11, #28 |
| HD-R016 | Nursing homes are not in POS_OTHER; FQHCs are; POS_OTHER holds only categories 01, 06, 12, 19, 21 (44,429 rows) | 0 HHA, hospice, ESRD, SNF rows | hunch_matrix_refined #25; hunch_expansion_32_75 #74 |
| HD-R017 | OTP NPI column holds space-separated multiples | split before joining | hunch_matrix_refined #4 |
| HD-R018 | FED_CFPB_HMDA (28k) and _LAR (17k) are samples; use HISTORIC (45M, 2015–2017 all actions) | samples fake rates | hunch_matrix_refined #5 |
| HD-R019 | TRI FAC_LATITUDE is DDMMSS text on 48,566 rows; PREF_LONGITUDE stored positive; only 36,721 of 64,990 have decimal coords; HOLC_GRADE 814 blanks and trailing spaces; use try_to_geography | one bad polygon throws Invalid Lng/Lat | hunch_matrix_refined #22 |
| HD-R020 | EPA corporate crosswalk null-parent bucket fakes 681 lenders → 1,807 sites; filter ULTIMATE_PARENT_LEI is not null | phantom matches | hunch_matrix_refined #3 |
| HD-R021 | DME supplier file has no year column, ingested 2026-07-26; dollars = TOT_SUPLR_SRVCS × AVG_SUPLR_MDCR_PYMT_AMT per HCPCS row; grain supplier × HCPCS × rental flag | say the vintage before publishing | hunch_matrix_refined #23 |
| HD-R022 | USASpending TOTAL_DOLLARS_OBLIGATED is a lifetime total repeated per action row; use max per CONTRACT_AWARD_UNIQUE_KEY and distinct UEIs | first pass read $314M over 154 rows | hunch_matrix_refined #15 |
| HD-R023 | FEMA IA registrations carry no applicant identity; SAM ∩ FEMA assistance CFDA 97 = 0 | dead legs | hunch_matrix_refined #15 |
| HD-R024 | Pending-provider union has 17 duplicate NPIs; dedupe before joining | Louisville's 2 rows read as 4 | hunch_matrix_refined #27 |
| HD-R025 | VERA later years are partial, not zero; a drop after 2019 is coverage | 2020+ thins to 597 counties by 2026; WA 39→16→8 | hunch_matrix_refined #8; hunch_expansion_32_75 #35 |
| HD-R026 | PENALTY_DATE starts 2023-06-17; deficiencies go back to 2017; do not mix windows | first penalty means first since then | hunch_matrix_refined #2, #30 |
| HD-R027 | Read the table name as the mart unless it says LANDING; several LANDING names are stubs (FED_FDA_DEVICE_510K 88, FED_FDA_MAUDE 1,386, FED_FDA_GUDID 2,542) and some LANDING tables are bigger (FORM5500_FULL 4.3M vs 33k, NADAC 1.5M vs 359k, FARA_BULK 222k vs 48k) | check both before calling a gap | hunch_matrix_refined § Appendix 2 |
| HD-R028 | Senate LDA filings and Google political ads sit in LIBRARY_MARTS.EDUCATION; registry domain column lies on at least 12 rows | misfiled tables | hunch_matrix_refined § Appendix 2; docket_v2_notes § Misfiled |
| HD-R029 | A column can exist and be empty; nothing in a column-name check proves fill | the appendix blind spot | hunch_matrix_refined § Skeptic verdict on this appendix |
| HD-R030 | Name-only matches are noise unless multi-word or address-confirmed; verify individually (2026-09-03 surname-leak trap) | excluded-entity vs FEC joins | hunch_matrix_refined § Appendix 1 |
| HD-R031 | House disbursements: 7.6% subtotal rows hold 75% of the naive dollar sum; filter DESCRIPTION LIKE '%TOTALS%'; AMOUNT is text with six column-shifted rows; SOD_QUARTER has 42 spellings | subtotals double count | hunch_matrix_refined cards 34, 92 |
| HD-R032 | FED_HOUSE_FD_PTR_INDEX DOCID not unique (41,883 rows / 41,860); dedupe on a full-row hash | double counting | hunch_matrix_refined card 35 |
| HD-R033 | Open Payments payer id column named PAYMENT is an ID, never sum it | misleading name | hunch_matrix_refined card 85 |
| HD-R034 | Google POLADS: use SPEND_USD only, other 20 currency columns zero-filled for US rows | wrong totals | hunch_matrix_refined card 86 |
| HD-R035 | FARA EMPLOYER is dirty free text, never a key | name noise | hunch_matrix_refined card 87 |
| HD-R036 | Committee membership is a current snapshot with no term dates; a 2019 trade against a 2026 roster is wrong (now covers 113-119, one row per congress, select distinct) | fans out 5.7x | hunch_matrix_refined cards 78, 84; docket_combined 84 |
| HD-R037 | SUP_OPP CSV is the source of truth for independent expenditure for/against; pas2 and oth 24A/24E cross-check only | wrong sign | hunch_matrix_refined card 84 |
| HD-R038 | EAVS: blank, 'not applicable' and zero are three different things; 430 coded columns, no codebook | misread | hunch_matrix_refined card 93 |
